if not bit32 then bit32 = {} end
if not bit32.bxor then
    bit32.bxor = function(a, b)
        local r, bitv = 0, 1
        while a > 0 or b > 0 do
            local ab, bb = a % 2, b % 2
            if ab ~= bb then r = r + bitv end
            a = (a - ab) / 2
            b = (b - bb) / 2
            bitv = bitv * 2
        end
        return r
    end
end

local QFK8X7SQps = "αβγδεζηθικλμνξοπρστυφχψω的一是不了人我在有他这为之大来以个中上们कखगघचछजझटठडढणतथदधनपफबभमयरलवशषसह"
local function u0wlk7cOmx(encoded, key)
    local clean = {}
    local ci = 0
    local n = #encoded
    local kk = 1
    while kk <= n do
        local byte1 = string.byte(encoded, kk)
        if byte1 and byte1 < 128 and QFK8X7SQps:find(encoded:sub(kk, kk), 1, true) == nil then
            ci = ci + 1
            clean[ci] = encoded:sub(kk, kk)
            kk = kk + 1
        elseif byte1 and byte1 < 128 then
            kk = kk + 1
        else
            if byte1 >= 240 then kk = kk + 4
            elseif byte1 >= 224 then kk = kk + 3
            elseif byte1 >= 192 then kk = kk + 2
            else kk = kk + 1 end
        end
    end
    local cleaned = table.concat(clean):gsub('=', '')
    local bytes = {}
    local bn = 0
    local p = 1
    local clen = #cleaned
    while p <= clen do
        local c1 = LJvMVv1Gsj[cleaned:sub(p, p)] or 0
        local c2 = LJvMVv1Gsj[cleaned:sub(p + 1, p + 1)] or 0
        local c3raw = cleaned:sub(p + 2, p + 2)
        local c4raw = cleaned:sub(p + 3, p + 3)
        local c3 = LJvMVv1Gsj[c3raw]
        local c4 = LJvMVv1Gsj[c4raw]
        local b1 = (c1 * 4) + math.floor(c2 / 16)
        bn = bn + 1; bytes[bn] = b1 % 256
        if c3 ~= nil then
            local b2 = ((c2 % 16) * 16) + math.floor(c3 / 4)
            bn = bn + 1; bytes[bn] = b2 % 256
            if c4 ~= nil then
                local b3 = ((c3 % 4) * 64) + c4
                bn = bn + 1; bytes[bn] = b3 % 256
            end
        end
        p = p + 4
    end
    local klen = #key
    local out = {}
    for idx = 1, bn do
        local kchar = key:byte(((idx - 1) % klen) + 1)
        out[idx] = string.char(bit32.bxor(bytes[idx], kchar))
    end
    return table.concat(out)
end

local function zguCv6FlAv(salt, length)
    local mb = {}
    for i = 1, #D_1L6qzSn8 do mb[i] = D_1L6qzSn8:byte(i) end
    local n = #mb
    local out = {}
    for j = 0, length - 1 do
        local idx = ((salt + j) % n) + 1
        local v = (mb[idx] + salt * 7 + j * 3) % 95 + 32
        out[j + 1] = string.char(v)
    end
    return table.concat(out)
end
